### [1] [A Large-Scale Technosignature Survey of the Anti-Solar Point with the Allen Telescope Array: Survey Design and Pilot Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17376)
*Sofia Z. Sheikh,Eleanor Hort,Bárbara Cabrales,Isabel Gerrard,Wael Farah,Nick Tusay,David R. DeBoer,Saul Balcarcel-Salazar,Joe S. Bright,Luigi F. Cruz,Ben Jacobson-Bell,Mark Masters,Alexander W. Pollak,Seth Shostak,Carol Shumaker,Andrew Siemion*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一项利用艾伦望远镜阵列（ATA）在反太阳点（ASP）搜寻射电技术信号的初步观测活动，未发现窄带信号，给出了通量密度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高搜寻地外文明技术信号（technosignatures）的效率，需聚焦于宇宙中可能被选作通信时机的‘谢林点’（Schelling Points），其中反太阳点（ASP）是一个有物理意义的候选位置——此时地球恰好位于太阳与潜在发射源之间，且发射源处于地球凌日区（ecliptic）。

Method: 使用艾伦望远镜阵列（ATA）对反太阳点方向开展射电观测；在2023年夏季进行了为期三晚的先导性观测，覆盖频率1–2.4 GHz，RA范围约20.266–21.312小时，并进行窄带信号搜寻。

Result: 未在观测数据中检测到天空定位的窄带射电技术信号；据此设定了115–936 Jy的通量密度上限（取决于频率和时间）。

Conclusion: 该先导研究验证了在ASP开展系统性技术信号搜寻的可行性，虽未发现信号，但为后续更大规模、更长时间的观测提供了方法基础与灵敏度基准。

Abstract: In the search for technosignatures, it can be useful to narrow down the cosmic haystack by prioritizing time-coordinate ``Schelling Points'' at which a beacon may be more likely to be transmitted. One such Schelling Point is the Anti-Solar Point (ASP) the place and moment at which Earth would lie directly between a hypothetical transmitter in the Earth Transit Zone (i.e., ecliptic) and the Sun. In this work, we describe a campaign to survey the ASP for radio technosignatures using the Allen Telescope Array, report the results of three initial nights of data from a pilot study in Summer 2023, and discuss the future of the campaign. In the pilot study, we find no sky-localized narrowband 1-2.4 GHz technosignatures within an RA range of approximately 20.266-21.312 hrs during the ASP, setting an upper limit flux density threshold of 115-936 Jy.

</details>


### [2] [Simple multi-interacting fluids in SymBoltz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17384)
*David Figueruelo,Herman Sletmoen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Einstein-Boltzmann代码SymBoltz中简洁、通用地实现重子、暗物质与暗能量之间任意相互作用的方法，依托其符号-数值接口和无近似结构，仅需几行代码即可定义协变耦合，显著简化复杂相互作用模型的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Einstein-Boltzmann代码（如CLASS、CAMB）在实现多组分（重子、暗物质、暗能量）间复杂相互作用时实现繁琐、修改困难；亟需一种更简洁、灵活、可扩展的建模框架。

Method: 基于SymBoltz的符号-数值混合架构，直接输入协变耦合形式，自动生成无近似、刚性但稳定的常微分方程组，并利用符号推导得到稀疏且解析的ODE雅可比矩阵，配合高效隐式求解器进行积分。

Result: 单相互作用模型验证显示，背景演化及物质/CMB功率谱与修改版CLASS结果在多数尺度上达成0.1%–1%一致性；多组分同步相互作用模型仅需约300行代码，新增交互仅需3行定义。

Conclusion: SymBoltz提供了一种高度简洁、鲁棒且易扩展的工具，大幅降低了宇宙学相互作用模型的实现门槛，为探索新物理提供了便捷平台。

Abstract: We implement a simple and general way of specifying arbitrary interactions between baryons, dark matter and dark energy in the Einstein--Boltzmann code SymBoltz. Due to the symbolic-numeric interface and approximation-free structure of SymBoltz, the model definition is remarkably compact and convenient to modify by specifying only the covariant coupling. The full equations are stiff, but stable and fast to integrate with an efficient implicit solver, particularly with the sparse and analytical ODE Jacobian generated from the symbolic equations. We first validate it using single-interaction models, comparing their background evolution and matter and CMB power spectra with those from modified versions of CLASS, finding $0.1\%$--$1\%$ agreement at most scales. Finally, we show how our framework makes it trivial to implement models with any interactions of any complexity among all three species simultaneously, which requires a more involved implementation in other Einstein--Boltzmann codes like CLASS or CAMB. Our full model is specified in only around 300 lines of code and is easy to modify for any other interaction by just specifying it in three lines.

</details>


### [3] [Mapping the Evolving Cosmic Web on the Lightcone with WebON: HOLI Mock Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17385)
*Francisco-Shu Kitaura,Francesco Sinigaglia,Kilian Peraza Vera,Pere Rosselló,Daniel Forero-Sánchez,Natalia Villa-Nova Rodrigues,Jorge E. García-Farieta,Yunyi Tang,Jose María Coloma-Nadal,Ginevra Favole,Aurelio Carnero-Rosell,Cheng Zhao,Kentaro Nagamine*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为WebON的快速方法，用于在观测者过去光锥上映射演化中的暗物质宇宙网，无需周期性盒子复制或分层快照拼接；该方法结合Zel'dovich近似与连续红移校正，在单次前向计算中生成光锥位置，并支持ALPT/eALPT引力求解器及短程ridging修正，验证了其在全天空物质与宇宙网演化模拟中角功率谱的百分比级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽视场光谱巡天（如DESI）需要大量遵循引力演化和示踪体分布的光锥模拟星表，而传统方法依赖周期性盒子复制或快照分层拼接，效率低且引入人为边界效应。

Method: WebON利用有限红移处计算的位移场，结合粒子级Zel'dovich发射红移估计与连续红移校正，在单次前向传递中生成光锥位置；支持正则化ALPT/eALPT引力求解器，并可选加短程ridging修正；还可集成相对论位置修正与局域原初非高斯性模块。

Result: 在全天空物质与宇宙网演化模拟中，光锥与固定红移角功率谱在测试红移与多极范围内达到百分比级一致；ridged LPT物质场与Abacus N体模拟及层级示踪体偏置模型的已有百分比级测试结果相互印证。

Conclusion: WebON是一种高效、自洽且高精度的光锥宇宙网建模方法，为DESI等巡天提供可靠、可扩展的模拟基础，并通过与HOLI策略的谱宇宙网层次关联，支持更高精度的示踪体聚类建模。

Abstract: Wide-area spectroscopic surveys such as DESI require large ensembles of mock catalogues that follow gravitational evolution and tracer populations across the observer's past lightcone. We present WebON (Web ON the lightcone), a fast method for mapping the evolving dark-matter cosmic web onto the lightcone without periodic-box replication or the stitching of separately evolved snapshot shells. Displacements computed at a limited set of redshifts are combined with a particle-wise Zel'dovich estimate of the emission redshift and a continuous-redshift correction, allowing lightcone positions to be generated in a single forward pass. The method supports regularised ALPT and eALPT gravity solvers, with an optional short-range ridging correction. We illustrate full-sky matter and cosmic-web evolution and find percent-level agreement between lightcone and fixed-redshift angular power spectra over the tested redshift and multipole ranges, providing an internal consistency check of the lightcone construction. Optional modules address approximate relativistic position corrections and local primordial non-Gaussianity. Finally, we show how the spectral cosmic-web hierarchy connects WebON to the HOLI (Higher Order LIghtcone) mock strategy, in which post-processing ridging and calibrated HICOBIAN bias and subgrid prescriptions can recover tracer clustering beyond the nominal resolution of the ALPT matter field. Independent comparisons of ridged LPT matter fields with Abacus N -body simulations and published percent-level tests of the hierarchical tracer-bias model complement the lightcone consistency measurements presented here.

</details>

Urbano,C. Valieri,A. Venhola,D. Vergani,P. Vielzeuf,A. Viitanen,E. Vilenius,S. Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,N. A. Walton,L. Wang,A. H. Wright,H. W. Yeung,D. Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得（Euclid）卫星任务数据发布1（DR1）中MERge处理函数（MER PF）的设计与实现，用于在多波段数据中检测天体、测量其性质并生成统一星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持欧几里得任务科学目标，需将空间（VIS/NISP）与地面（ugriz）多波段成像数据融合，以精确测定测光红移和星系性质，因此开发了MER PF流程。

Method: MER PF采用多种测光方法：Kron总流量、PSF匹配图像上的孔径测光、模板拟合测光和Sérsic轮廓拟合测光，并辅以形态学测量与质量标识；通过内部比对与处理验证确保数据一致性与可靠性。

Result: 成功构建了DR1多波段联合星表，包含经验证的天体检测、测光与形态参数，并完成了测光均匀性评估及不同处理方案的系统性对比。

Conclusion: MER PF是欧几里得DR1数据处理的核心环节，其模块化设计与多方法协同保障了星表的科学完备性与公开发布质量，为后续宇宙学分析奠定基础。

Abstract: The ESA satellite mission \Euclid was launched in July 2023 and started its regular observing mode in February 2024. The data from the \Euclid instruments Visible Camera (VIS) and the Near IR Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) are complemented by ground-based imaging data in the $u$, $g$ $r$, $i$, and $z$ bands to enable the determination of photometric redshifts and other galaxy properties. This paper describes the MERge processing function (MER PF) that has been developed and run for the Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1). The aim of the MER PF is to detect objects in the \Euclid imaging data, measure their properties, and combine them into a single multi-wavelength catalogue. In this paper we illustrate the design of the MER PF and introduce the different photometric methods applied to the data, which are Kron total flux, aperture photometry on Point Spread Functions (PSF) -matched images, template fitting photometry, and Sérsic fitting photometry. We also present the various morphological measurements and quality flags that improve the scientific content of the output catalogues and better characterize the detected sources. We illustrate the processing and measurements that help to validate the MER PF results, which is an essential step to qualify the output for the public DR1 release. We discuss the global assessment of the photometric homogeneity and provide an internal comparison of our processing results. In addition, we give an extensive overview of the DR1 output data and the various processing scenarios

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 AstroVink-TREX 的神经网络，用于自动分离强引力透镜图像中的透镜光、源光及干扰成分，从而支持大规模引力透镜质量建模。该网络采用混合架构（ViT 编码器 + 卷积分支）和定制损失函数，输出三通道通量图及建模所需全部辅助信息（如中心、拟合区域、共轭点等），已在 Euclid DR1 超过 3000 个系统中验证其高可靠性（仅 39 例因分割错误导致建模失败），实现了从人工预处理到全自动建模的跨越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid 引力透镜建模依赖人工图像成分分离，难以应对 DR1 中超 15,000 个候选体的大规模需求，亟需自动化方法。

Method: 提出 AstroVink-TREX 神经网络：融合复用自透镜检测网络的视觉Transformer编码器与并行卷积分支；设计兼顾通量误差与结构误差、支持各成分独立加权的定制损失函数；从预测通量图中进一步推导建模中心、拟合区域、共轭点和二值掩膜等关键输入。

Result: 在约 3000 个 DR1 系统的专家评估中（75% 一致率），仅 39 个因分割错误导致建模失败；该网络已为 DR1 全部建模系统提供自动成分分离与建模设置。

Conclusion: AstroVink-TREX 实现了强引力透镜图像成分分离的全自动化，显著提升建模效率与可扩展性，使建模能力与透镜发现规模相匹配。

Abstract: Strong gravitational lens modelling requires the lens light, lensed source light, and contaminants to be separated in each image before mass modelling codes can fit the system. Within Euclid this separation has so far been done by hand, which does not scale to the more than 15,000 candidates in the Euclid Lens Identification Pipeline and Scientific Exploration (ECLIPSE) catalogue of Data Release 1 (DR1). We present AstroVink-TREX , a neural network that performs this separation automatically and predicts three flux maps, in a standard format directly usable by mass-modelling pipelines. The network uses a hybrid architecture, pairing a vision transformer encoder reused from the lens detection network with a parallel convolutional branch. A custom loss function penalises both flux and structural error, while allowing independent weighting per component. From the network's predictions we also derive the modelling centre, fitting region, conjugate points, and binary maps, providing all the inputs that a modelling pipeline needs to run without manual setup. We evaluate the efficiency of the network on DR1 systems against existing fits and through expert validation. Across approximately 3000 inspected systems, taking 75% agreement between inspectors, just 39 lens models fail because of a segmentation error. Following this validation, the network provides the component separation and modelling setup for every system modelled in DR1. With automated segmentation, lens modelling is no longer limited by manual preprocessing and can now operate at the same scale as lens discovery.

</details>

Conclusion: PyVesta 成功实现了对多种光纤阶梯光谱仪的通用、高效、自动化数据还原，尤其推动了低成本光谱设备在科研中的实用化与标准化。

Abstract: We present PyVesta, an automatic data reduction software for fiber fed echelle spectrographs. PyVesta is designed to be used with different instruments, both high resolution spectrographs such as HARPS or FEROS as well as inexpensive off-the-shelf spectrographs like the Shelyak eShel II. Special additions were implemented to improve the stability and resolution of inexpensive instruments especially. PyVesta provides tools for all extraction steps from raw CCD images to continuum normalized spectra, e.g., CCD image corrections, spectra extraction, wavelength calibration, and continuum normalization. Our software is based on the software package CERES and was ported to Python 3, revised, and supplemented.

</details>

<details>

Method: 扩展了原有的加性对数正态分层模型，引入半方差校正以匹配光子计数的乘性物理模型；构建能融合仪器、源和波段间信息的贝叶斯分层模型，使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）进行拟合。

Conclusion: 所提贝叶斯分层模型能有效整合多源异构观测数据，提升空间仪器校准精度与可解释性，为天体物理测量提供稳健的统计框架。

Abstract: The ongoing calibration of space-based instruments is essential to ensure accurate measurements of physical quantities such as photon flux. The goal is to obtain a unified photon-flux measurement of a single astronomical source using data obtained with multiple instruments. Calibration involves in-flight adjustments to address discrepancies arising from instrument-specific variations. Taking a statistical approach, we extend a previously proposed additive log-Normal hierarchical model with half-variance correction based on a multiplicative physical model for photon counts. The newly proposed Bayesian hierarchical model can leverage domain-specific priors to account for correlated passbands and integrate information across different instruments, sources, and passbands effectively. The model is fitted using Markov chain Monte Carlo and can provide uncertainty quantification for instrument- and source-specific parameter estimation. Moreover, we obtain valid inferences for other structural parameters of the physics model. We use data derived from Chandra and XMM-Newton X-ray observatories to illustrate the proposed inference procedure.

</details>


### [8] [bihalofit-JAX: A Differentiable Code for the Nonlinear Matter Bispectrum and its Projected Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18281)
*Alexander Roskill,Matteo Zennaro*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了bihalofit-JAX，一个基于BiHalofit拟合公式的开源、完全可微分的非线性物质三谱计算代码，显著提升了计算速度，并支持多种投影统计量，适用于宇宙学参数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，非线性物质三谱及其投影统计量的理论预测计算代价高昂，导致许多分析依赖于代理模型（emulator）加速；本文旨在通过改进数值技术实现快速、直接的预测评估，摆脱对emulator的依赖。

Method: 开发了bihalofit-JAX——一个基于JAX实现的、完全可微分的BiHalofit代码，支持向量化与自动微分；优化了数值算法，大幅提高三谱及角三谱、偏斜谱、孔径统计等投影统计量的计算效率。

Result: 在单个宇宙学模型下计算10000个三角形的物质三谱仅需~1–2 ms，比其他实现快1–3个数量级；对500个宇宙学模型批量计算仅需~2 ms；第三阶孔径统计计算提速达3×10³–2.4×10⁴倍；数值精度优于原BiHalofit代码0.02%，且已成功应用于NUTS与emcee的梯度/无梯度推断对比实验。

Conclusion: bihalofit-JAX证明了高精度、高速、可微分的三谱计算是可行的，为基于高阶统计量的宇宙学参数推断提供了高效可靠的新工具。

Abstract: Theoretical predictions for the nonlinear matter bispectrum and related projected statistics have been historically very expensive to evaluate, leading many analyses to rely on emulators to speed up slow numerical codes. We show in this paper that this need not be the case: improved numerical techniques can be leveraged to directly evaluate predictions for the nonlinear matter bispectrum and projected statistics at speeds preferable for inference. We present bihalofit-JAX, an open-source, fully differentiable code for the nonlinear matter bispectrum based on the BiHalofit fitting formula, which includes numerous related projected statistics such as angular bispectra, skew spectra and aperture statistics. It computes $10000$ triangles of the matter bispectrum in $\sim1$--$2\ \rm{ms}$ per cosmology, one to three orders of magnitude faster than other implementations. It agrees numerically with the original BiHalofit code to better than $0.02\%$ on average over the cosmologies the formula was calibrated on, where the formula itself is accurate to $\sim20\%$ against simulations. It is also efficiently vectorised such that one can compute e.g. $500$ cosmologies in $\sim 2\ \rm{ms}$. This speed-up grows even more in the case of the third-order aperture statistics, which we compute in $\sim 6\ \rm{ms}$ for one cosmology, between $3\times10^3$ and $2.4\times10^4$ times faster than other publicly available codes. The pipeline is fully differentiable, and we demonstrate its performance in a simple inference problem, comparing the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) to gradient-free inference with the affine-invariant ensemble sampler (emcee).

</details>

Abstract: To evaluate the potential of detectors for next-generation space astrometry missions such as the HWO and Theia, an extensive analysis of the performance of the GIGAPYX-4600 46-megapixel CMOS detector from Pyxalis was conducted at IPAG. The 5456x8320-pixel detector, featuring a 4.4-micron pitch and back-side illumination, constitutes a matrix of 24x36 mm. It has a measured gain of 13.3 electrons per ADU in low-gain mode. The saturation level of the detector in low-gain-mode is estimated to be approximately 52 000 electrons. This sensor demonstrates linearity errors of 0.90%, pixel response non-uniformity of less than 1.3%, and readout and quantization noise of 12 electrons in low-gain mode. Dark current was measured at 35 electrons per second at 35{\textdegree}C and inter-pixel capacitance remained under 3%. We demonstrated that inter-pixel capacitance effects can be effectively mitigated using spatially varying kernel correction algorithms. Radiation tests confirmed its robustness against protons and heavy ions, and no latch-up was observed. These measurements agree with the manufacturer figures. These characterization results validate the GIGAPYX-4600 as a strong candidate for high-precision astrometry. Further improvements are currently being developed for space implementation.

</details>

Abstract: Complementary metal-oxide semiconductor (CMOS) image sensors offer distinct advantages as imaging detectors for space-based X-ray observatories, being low-power, radiation-resistant, and operable at room temperature. Previous characterizations of the low-cost, commercially-available IMX290LLR-C back-illuminated CMOS sensor from Sony Semiconductor Solutions have found Fano-limited X-ray spectral resolutions while operated at room temperature and a radiation tolerance compatible with use in low Earth orbit. Here, we report the quantum efficiency of the IMX290LLR-C as measured across the soft X-ray band (200 - 1000 eV) using Beamline 6.3.2 of the Advanced Light Source at Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory. We find a quantum efficiency of roughly 10 -- 40\% over this band, and fit these measurements to a quantum efficiency model constrained by these measurements and microscopy of the sensor layers. These measurements and the derived model informs this sensor's application within low-cost X-ray observatories, as well as motivates the development of commercial X-ray CMOS sensors without microlenses and deeper Si depletion layers.

</details>

### [11] [Holographically motivated Hubble-running dark energy: Phantom-divide crossing in VCDM and observational constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17374)
*Antonio De Felice,Burin Gumjudpai,Shinji Tsujikawa,Jack Zidich*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全息暗能量构造的Hubble-running状态方程（HoloEoS），将其嵌入到仅传播张量模的VCDM引力理论中，并通过CMB、BAO和超新星等观测数据进行约束，发现其能自然实现暗能量状态方程穿越幽灵边界（w=-1），且拟合优度与CPL参数化相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受全息暗能量模型中暗能量状态方程对哈勃参量呈幂律依赖的启发，研究一种形式为 w_DE = w0 (H/H0)^q 的动态状态方程。

Method: 将HoloEoS嵌入VCDM（一类II型最小修改引力理论）框架，重构其辅助标量场势函数 V(φ)，从而获得背景演化与宇宙扰动的拉格朗日描述；利用SPT、Planck、ACT、DESI DR2及DESY5（Dovekie校准）等多组观测数据进行贝叶斯分析与MH模拟退火优化。

Result: 对联合数据，得到 w0 = -0.881^{+0.040}_{-0.038}，q = 0.453^{+0.143}_{-0.133}，穿越红移 z× = 0.476^{+0.106}_{-0.081}；AIC比较显示HoloEoS相对于ΛCDM有ΔAIC ≈ -3.40，优于ΛCDM且与CPL-VCDM（ΔAIC ≈ -4.10）性能相近。

Conclusion: HoloEoS在保持广义相对论张量自由度的前提下，可自然实现w < -1的晚时演化并跨越幽灵边界，其观测拟合能力媲美主流参数化模型，为暗能量本质提供了具物理动机的新候选方案。

Abstract: Motivated by power-law dependences on the expansion scale in holographic dark-energy constructions, we study the Hubble-running equation of state $w_{\rm DE}=w_0(H/H_0)^q$ (HoloEoS), where $H$ is the Hubble expansion rate, $H_0$ and $w_0$ are the present values of $H$ and $w_{\rm DE}$, and $q$ is a constant dimensionless exponent controlling the running. We embed this history in VCDM, a type-II minimally modified gravity theory that propagates only the two tensor modes of general relativity. For regular expanding histories with standard matter, we reconstruct the potential $V(φ)$ of its nondynamical auxiliary scalar field, providing a Lagrangian description of both the homogeneous background and cosmological perturbations. This framework allows HoloEoS to realize a smooth late-time crossing of the phantom divide $w_{\rm DE}=-1$. We confront this VCDM realization with SPT, Planck, and ACT CMB data, updated DESI DR2 distances, and optionally DESY5 supernovae with Dovekie recalibration, comparing it with $Λ$CDM and the Chevallier--Polarski--Linder parameterization embedded in the same gravitational framework (CPL-VCDM). For the full data combination, the posterior medians and central $68\%$ credible intervals are $w_0=-0.881^{+0.040}_{-0.038}$ and $q=0.453^{+0.143}_{-0.133}$, with a crossing redshift $z_\times=0.476^{+0.106}_{-0.081}$ conditional on $0<z_\times<6$. For this data combination, physical-likelihood optimization by Metropolis--Hastings (MH) simulated annealing gives Akaike information criterion differences of $Δ\mathrm{AIC}\simeq-3.40$ for HoloEoS and $-4.10$ for CPL-VCDM relative to $Λ$CDM. Thus, a Hubble-scale-motivated dark-energy history realized within a specified gravitational theory achieves fitting performance comparable to CPL-VCDM while naturally allowing phantom-divide crossing.

</details>


### [12] [Full-shape analysis of the Lyman-alpha forest power spectrum and bispectrum: validation on simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17405)
*Roger de Belsunce,James M. Sullivan,Mikhail M. Ivanov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Lyα森林功率谱、交叉功率谱及三谱的有效场论模型，用于DESI观测数据的全形状分析，显著提升了宇宙学参数（如H₀、Ωₘ、σ₈）的约束精度，尤其通过引入bispectrum缓解了Lyα特有的简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为从DESI等高红移巡天中提取更多宇宙学信息，需突破传统仅用功率谱的限制，解决Lyα森林中平均通量与功率谱振幅之间的简并问题。

Method: 构建包含Lyα森林自功率谱（FF）、Lyα-类星体交叉功率谱（FQ）的一环修正项，以及Lyα三谱（FFF）的树级项的有效场论模型；在AbacusSummit模拟上进行验证，并联合拟合不同k_max截断下的功率谱与三谱。

Result: 在8 h⁻³ Gpc³模拟体积中，H₀、Ωₘ、σ₈的测量精度分别达0.6%、1.6%、3.5%；加入FFF三谱使σ₈约束提升28%；进一步加入类星体QQ与QQQ谱可再提升约35%。

Conclusion: 该EFT框架支持对DESI及DESI-II高红移数据开展全形状分析，显著增强对物质主导时期结构增长的理解与约束能力。

Abstract: The Lyman-$α$ (LyA) forest of neutral hydrogen provides a large-volume, high-resolution map of cosmological fluctuations from optical spectra at high redshift. To extract more information from the currently observing Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), we derive an effective field theory (EFT) model for joint full-shape analyses of the LyA forest data. It combines the one-loop power spectrum of the LyA forest (FF), and of its cross-correlation with dark-matter halos, used as proxies for high-redshift quasars (FQ), with the tree-level bispectrum of the LyA forest (FFF). Importantly, the bispectrum breaks the LyA-specific degeneracy between the mean flux and the amplitude of the power spectrum. We validate our theory framework on idealized large-volume LyA forest mocks painted onto the AbacusSummit N-body simulation suite at redshift z=2.5. Fitting scales up to $k_\mathrm{max}^{FF\,(FQ)}=0.6\,(0.3)\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ in the power spectrum and $k_\mathrm{max}^{FFF}=0.1\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ in the bispectrum, our full-shape analysis yields unbiased constraints on the cosmological parameters in simulations. For a fully sampled flux field in a simulated volume of $V=8\,h^{-3}\,{\rm Gpc}^3$, we measure the Hubble constant, $H_0$, to $0.6\%$, the matter density, $Ω_{\rm m}$, to $1.6\%$ and the amplitude of matter clustering, $σ_8$, to $3.5\%$. Adding the bispectrum improves the constraints on $σ_8$ by $28\%$. We also demonstrate that the inclusion of the quasar one-loop power spectrum (QQ) and tree-level bispectrum (QQQ) further improves cosmological constraints by $\approx 35\%$ using $k_\mathrm{max}^{QQ,\,(QQQ)}=0.2,\,(0.08)\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$. Our theory framework enables full-shape analyses of high-redshift data from DESI and its successor DESI-II, enhancing knowledge of the growth of structure of the Universe during matter-domination.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过消融研究评估了Euclid巡天中强引力透镜自动探测的CNN模型性能，重点分析了训练集构成、图像表示方式（VIS-only vs. pseudo-RGB）、数据格式（FITS vs. JPEG）、难例挖掘和多阶段训练等因素的影响，并验证了集成方法在真实DR1数据上的显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid巡天将发现大量强引力透镜，亟需高完备率、低误报率的自动化探测方法；但当前深度学习模型在巡天尺度下的训练策略影响仍不明确。

Method: 对ECLIPSE检测流程中的CNN模型开展系统性消融实验，涵盖图像表示（VIS-only / pseudo-RGB）、数据格式（FITS / JPEG）、训练集组成（含模拟预训练）、难负样本选择、多阶段训练及logit-mean集成，在统一测试集和约6700万DR1切片上评估性能。

Result: 模拟预训练缺失导致高召回率下加权平均精度下降74.5%–83.7%；难负样本选择显著影响性能；FITS比JPEG在高召回下仅提升2–5%；logit-mean集成使平均精度提升28.0%–39.9%，并在约7万候选体中识别出11256个A/B级透镜（优于单模型）。

Conclusion: 训练集设计、真实负样本构建与多波段集成对巡天级透镜搜寻至关重要；最终集成模型在运行阈值下达到约80%召回率，优于全局指标更高的消融模型，表明应针对实际巡天条件优化性能。

Abstract: Euclid is expected to discover ~$10^5$ galaxy-galaxy strong gravitational lenses across ~14,000deg$^2$, requiring automated searches with high completeness and low false-positive rates. Despite advances in deep learning, the effects of training-set composition, image representation, and preprocessing on survey-scale detection remain poorly understood. We present an ablation study of the convolutional neural network used in the ECLIPSE strong-lens detection pipeline, evaluating different training configurations on a fixed test set. Using simulated and real Euclid data, we compare VIS-only and pseudo-RGB representations, FITS and JPEG formats, training-set composition, hard-negative selection, and multi-stage training. We also assess ensembling on ~67 million DR1 cutouts. Training-set composition and strategy strongly affect high-Recall performance. Omitting simulation based pre-training reduces high-Recall weighted average Precision by 83.7% for VIS-only and 74.5% for pseudo-RGB models. Negative-sample selection also substantially affects performance, while FITS yields modest gains over JPEG at high Recall (~2-5%). On DR1, the logit-mean ensemble improves average Precision by 28.0% and 39.9% over the individual VIS-only and pseudo-RGB models. Among ~70,000 candidates selected for visual inspection, it recovers 11,256 unique grade A (secure) and B (probable) lenses, compared with 9650 and 10,395 for the individual models. The branches show complementary ranking preferences, particularly for lenses benefiting from near-infrared information. Careful training-set design, realistic negatives, and multi-band ensembling improve survey-scale searches. Our final ensemble achieves ~80% Recall at the operational selection threshold, outperforming ablation models with higher global classification metrics and highlighting the need to optimise performance for realistic survey conditions.[Abridged]

</details>

TL;DR: 本文分析了由弦理论启发的Fading Dark Sector (FDS) 模型，该模型通过一个标量场驱动暗能量与暗物质的演化，并用两个O(1)参数(c, c')刻画其密度对场的对数导数。研究将DESI DR1全形状星系功率谱数据纳入分析，发现FDS模型在拟合CMB、BAO、SN及结构增长数据方面优于CPL参数化和ΛCDM模型，且(c,c')值保持稳定，表明FDS对结构增长历史的预测更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受弦理论启发，旨在构建一个能自然解释暗能量与暗物质联合演化的统一暗区模型（FDS），并检验其对宇宙大尺度结构增长历史的预测能力。

Method: 基于FDS模型中两个O(1)参数(c, c')，结合DESI DR1全形状星系功率谱、CMB、DESI DR2 BAO及DES-Dovekie数据，进行贝叶斯参数估计与模型比较（Δχ²_MAP及显著性检验）。

Result: FDS模型在同等参数个数下较CPL参数化改善Δχ²_MAP = −4，且以4.1σ显著优于ΛCDM；引入全形状数据后(c, c')值未变，说明FDS对结构增长的预测具有一致性和优越性。

Conclusion: FDS模型不仅兼容现有宇宙学观测，还能更准确描述宇宙结构增长历史，是比标准w₀-wₐ参数化和ΛCDM更具物理动机与数据支持的暗区演化框架。

Abstract: We analyze the Fading Dark Sector (FDS) model, which is motivated by string theory and in which the fading of the dark sector is driven by a scalar field. The model is specified by two $O(1)$ numbers $(c,c')$ that parametrize the logarithmic derivatives of the dark energy and dark matter densities with respect to the field in Planck units. In previous work, we showed that this model provides a good fit to the DESI BAO, CMB, and SN datasets. Here, we extend that analysis to include the DESI DR1 full-shape galaxy power spectrum, which adds information about the time evolution of cosmic structure and provides an independent consistency check of the FDS model. We find that, when the DESI DR1 full-shape data are combined with CMB+DESI DR2 BAO+DES-Dovekie, the FDS model outperforms the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) $w_0$-$w_a$ parameterization by $Δχ^2_{\rm MAP}=-4$ at equal parameter count and is preferred over $Λ$CDM at $4.1σ$. The inferred values of $(c,c')$ with the full-shape data are unchanged from our previous analysis, so this improvement indicates that FDS makes a prediction for the growth of structure that captures the observed growth history better than the $w_0$-$w_a$ parameterization.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的方法，利用暗物质模拟数据和FLAMINGO流体模拟数据训练模型，以在大规模暗物质模拟中高效生成高保真度星系样本，克服了全流体模拟计算成本过高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全流体动力学模拟虽对宇宙学参数估计至关重要，但大尺度与高分辨率难以兼顾，计算代价过高；而暗物质模拟计算高效，需借助星系-暗晕关联模型（HOD等）生成星系样本，但传统方法难以精确刻画复杂物理过程。

Method: 使用极端随机树（ExtraTrees）建模中心星系的恒星质量分布，输入为暗晕的质量剖面、形成历史和环境特征；对卫星星系，采用高斯过程（GP）代理模型联合优化超参数并筛选对流体/暗物质模拟差异鲁棒的关键暗晕特征，再基于这些特征训练模型；最终将模型应用于2.8 Gpc的FLAMINGO-10k暗物质模拟，生成新星系星表。

Result: 生成的星系星表成功复现了训练所用小体积流体模拟预测的关键统计量：恒星质量函数、恒星质量–暗物质质量关系、两点相关函数，以及恒星质量与暗晕浓度、半质量半径、3 Mpc内暗物质质量的相关性。

Conclusion: 该方法能以远低于全流体模拟的计算成本，在大体积暗物质模拟中生成高保真星系样本，为未来大规模巡天的宇宙学分析提供了高效可靠的理论预测工具。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic simulations are instrumental in the analysis of observations such as galaxy clustering used for the estimation of cosmological parameters. However, for these purposes, the simulations need both a large box size and high resolution, which is computationally prohibitive. In this work we use the connection between galaxies and their host dark matter halos to populate dark-matter-only simulations, using data from the FLAMINGO simulations suite. We employ the Extremely Randomized Trees algorithm to model the connection between the mass profile, history and environment of dark matter halos and the probability distribution of the stellar mass of the galaxy contained in the halo. We use halos matched between a dark-matter-only simulation and the corresponding hydrodynamical simulation to train a model for centrals. For satellites, we employ a Gaussian Processes emulator that simultaneously optimises the hyperparameters of a model and selects for the dark matter features that are predictive of the stellar mass while being insensitive to the differences between the hydrodynamic and dark-matter-only simulations, then train a model on only these features. We then apply these models to produce a new galaxy catalogue for the 2.8 Gpc FLAMINGO-10k dark-matter-only simulation as a product of this work. We demonstrate that this catalogue reproduces the statistics of the galaxy-halo connection predicted by the smaller volume hydrodynamical simulation on which the model was trained, including the stellar mass function, stellar mass-dark matter mass relation, 2-point correlation functions and correlations between stellar mass and dark matter concentration, half mass radius and dark matter mass within 3 Mpc.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用IllustrisTNG流体模拟中的真实椭圆星系，研究了透镜质量模型中高阶角向多极结构（m=1,3,4）对哈勃常数H0测量的系统性影响；发现这些多极扰动普遍存在，忽略它们会导致1–6%的H0偏差，尤其奇数阶模式引发显著随机散射，并可能被外部剪切吸收造成‘隐藏偏差’；因此强调需引入多极先验并超越刚性参数模型以实现高精度宇宙测距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决哈勃常数测量中亚2%精度需求与质量模型系统误差之间的矛盾，探究真实星系中高阶多极结构对时间延迟宇宙测距的影响。

Method: 基于IllustrisTNG（TNG100-1和TNG50-1）模拟生成的真实大质量椭圆星系样本，分析m=1,3,4多极扰动的出现频率与强度，并通过贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）评估其建模必要性；定量模拟其对H0推断的偏差，并探讨其与外部剪切的简并性。

Result: 51%的样本中多极项建模更优；忽略~2%幅度的多极扰动导致H0偏差达1–2%（m=1）、4–5%（m=3）、3–6%（m=4）；m=4引起方向性偏差，m=1/m=3引发强随机散射；未建模多极可被外部剪切吸收，造成χ²≈1但H0严重偏移的‘隐藏偏差’。

Conclusion: 高阶多极结构是星系形成中自然涌现的关键系统误差源，必须在强引力透镜建模中予以考虑；应采用基于物理的多极先验，并发展更灵活的质量模型，以满足Euclid和LSST时代的高精度宇宙学要求。

Abstract: Time-delay cosmography is a powerful independent probe of the Hubble constant ($H_0$), yet reaching the sub-2$\%$ precision required to resolve the Hubble tension demands a rigorous treatment of mass model systematics. We use realistic massive elliptical galaxies from the IllustrisTNG hydrodynamic simulation suite to characterise higher-order angular mass complexity in the form of multipoles ($m=1,3,4$) and quantify their impact on $H_0$ inference. We find that these multipole perturbations are common and emergent features of galaxy assembly; in 51$\%$ of our total samples ($54\%$ TNG100-1 galaxies and $85\%$ for TNG50-1 galaxies), the addition of multipole terms to a standard elliptical power-law model is preferred by the Bayesian Information Criterion. Ignoring these structures in lens modelling introduces systematic biases in $H_0$: for perturbations of $\sim2\%$, the biases are of the order of 1--2$\%$ for $m=1$, 4--5$\%$ for $m=3$, and 3--6$\%$ for $m=4$ multipole. While the $m=4$ (boxy/discyness) mode induces a predictable directional bias, odd-order modes ($m=1,3$) introduce significant stochastic scatter. We demonstrate a degeneracy where unmodelled internal multipoles can be effectively absorbed by the external shear component. This combined with low signal-to-noise observations can create ``hidden biases'' where formally perfect fits to imaging data ($χ_ν^2 \approx 1$) could harbour significant cosmological errors. Our results provide an astrophysical basis for multipole priors and highlight the necessity of moving beyond rigid parametric models to achieve high-accuracy cosmography in the era of \textit{Euclid} and LSST.

</details>


### [17] [Note on quantum optimal measurements of CMB spectral distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17552)
*Roshni Singh,Jacques Delabrouille,Daniel Carney*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用量子传感技术测量宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）光谱畸变的可行性，通过量子Fisher信息分析证明最优策略是简单的频段强度测量，并估算了探测μ-和y-畸变所需的最小观测时间，同时将PIXIE和SPECTER任务与该理论极限进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子传感技术在超弱信号探测方面不断突破，作者思考是否可利用先进量子策略来测量宇宙微波背景（CMB）的光谱畸变。

Method: 采用量子Fisher信息方法，对CMB光谱畸变（μ-和y-畸变）的可探测性进行信息论分析，推导最优测量策略并估算最小观测时间。

Result: 证明最优测量策略为简单频段强度测量；给出ΛCDM模型下μ-和y-畸变探测所需的信息论最小观测时间；PIXIE和SPECTER任务性能距该极限仅差几个数量级。

Conclusion: 尽管量子传感前景广阔，但在CMB光谱畸变探测这一具体问题上，传统频段强度测量已接近信息论最优，无需更复杂量子策略。

Abstract: As quantum sensing techniques continue to push the frontier of detection of ultra-weak signals, one may wonder if a sophisticated quantum strategy could be used to enable measurement of spectral distortions in the cosmic microwave background. Using quantum Fisher information techniques, we prove that the optimal strategy is simple frequency-binned intensity measurement. This proof strategy also enables us to provide numerical estimates for the information-theoretic minimum observation time required to observe the $μ$- and $y$-distortions expected from $Λ$CDM cosmology. As case studies, we compare these results to the PIXIE and SPECTER mission concepts, and show that they are within a few orders of magnitude of this fundamental limit.

</details>

Motivation: 准确预测暗物质晕浓度参数对理解星系形成、弱引力透镜及宇宙大尺度结构至关重要；现有模型多针对统计平均，缺乏对单个晕浓度的高精度、普适性预测，尤其在WDM和高红shift下表现不足。

Result: 模型在所有测试宇宙学和红移下对松弛晕浓度预测散射约为0.09 dex，对全部晕为0.09–0.10 dex；z>3时全部晕散射增至0.18 dex，反映剧烈弛豫影响；固定质量下浓度散射主要源于并合历史；WDM中形成变量PDF比ΛCDM更窄，源于小尺度扰动抑制与近WDM截断尺度的快速延迟单体坍缩；蒙特卡洛实现能复现浓度分布并匹配先前峰浓度模型，而标准EPS低估延迟形成导致的浓度。

Conclusion: 所提模型是一个透明、计算轻量、跨宇宙学适用的工具，能高精度预测个体暗物质晕浓度；其成功依赖于对并合历史和WDM物理的显式建模，揭示了标准EPS理论在描述高红移和WDM晕形成中的局限性；为未来星系形成模拟与观测对比提供了关键先验。

Abstract: We present an empirical model for predicting concentration parameters of individual dark matter halos in cold and warm dark matter (WDM) cosmologies over z=0-5. Constructed using a large suite of cosmological N-body simulations, our model separately incorporates cosmic expansion (linear growth factor), mass scale (linear density fluctuations), halo mass assembly histories via the Extended Press-Schechter (EPS) scaled formation variable, plus a WDM damping term tied to the half-mode mass. It works for both virial and 200 $ρ_{crit}$ halo definitions. Across all tested cosmologies and redshifts, our global logarithmic fit gives concentration scatter ~0.09 dex for relaxed halos and ~0.09 to 0.10 dex for all halos. While relaxed halos maintain nearly constant scatter near 0.09 dex, the scatter of all halos at z>3 increases from 0.10 to 0.18 dex, revealing substantial violent-relaxation impacts on high-redshift halo structure. At fixed mass, concentration scatter is dominated by assembly history. We extract the scaled formation variable's probability distribution function (PDF) from merger trees and find departures from standard EPS theory. This PDF is narrower in WDM than LCDM due to suppressed small-scale perturbations and rapid, delayed monolithic collapse near the WDM truncation scale. Monte Carlo realizations with our formulae reproduce concentration distributions and match our prior peak-concentration model, whereas standard EPS underestimates concentrations from delayed-formation bias. Our framework offers a transparent, computationally lightweight tool to predict individual halo concentrations across diverse cosmological models.

</details>

Abstract: We constrain canonical axion quintessence with a negative cosmological constant using DES Year 5 supernovae, DESI DR2 BAO, compressed CMB information, and geometry-corrected Gold-2017 growth data. We solve the background and linear-growth equations and sample with PolyChord. Our joint prior is logarithmic in the decay constant over $0.005\leq f/M_{\rm Pl}\leq10$ and uniform in the initial angle $θ_i=φ_i/f\in[0,π]$, conditioned on $π-θ_i\geq H_{\rm inf}/(2πf)$ with $H_{\rm inf}=10^{13}$ GeV and reduced Planck mass $M_{\rm Pl}$. The broad-prior model favors recent thawing, with median $w_{{\rm DE},0}=-0.865^{+0.068}_{-0.053}$, $m_φ\in[0.91,4.39]\times10^{-33}$ eV, and $f/M_{\rm Pl}\in[0.589,4.899]$ (central 68\% credible intervals). Bayes factors relative to flat $Λ$CDM are 0.70 and 30.1 for the normalized fluctuation-conditioned prior (Convention I) and its further conditioning on numerical acceptance (Convention II). Restricting $f\leq M_{\rm Pl}$ gives $f/M_{\rm Pl}\in[0.262,0.822]$ at 68\% credibility and Bayes factors 0.233 and 32.4. Relative to the zero-vacuum axion model, the broad-prior Bayes factors are 0.620 and 1.65, providing no compelling evidence for a negative cosmological constant. Local likelihood optimization gives an Akaike information criterion difference $Δ{\rm AIC}\simeq-10.87$ for the zero-vacuum model relative to flat $Λ$CDM. For this model, assuming that the instanton generating $m_φ^2f^2=Λ_{\rm UV}^4e^{-S_{\rm inst}}$ satisfies the axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture, the one-sided 95\% posterior lower limit on the minimum compatible coefficient $c_{\min}$ rises from 37 to 55 as the ultraviolet scale $Λ_{\rm UV}$ increases from $10^{-10}M_{\rm Pl}$ to $M_{\rm Pl}$, in tension with an order-one coefficient under these assumptions.

</details>

Abstract: We study higher order correlation functions in single field inflation using the $δN$ formalism and methods from graph theory. By representing connected tree level contributions as graphs, we systematically classify their independent topologies, momentum shapes and amplitudes. We show that the amplitudes of arbitrary trees factorize into products of primary amplitudes, providing a general framework for relations among higher order correlators, including the Suyama-Yamaguchi equality. As the number of external legs increases, the number of distinct tree topologies grows exponentially, whereas the number of algebraically distinct amplitude products grows considerably more slowly. As an application, we consider ultra-slow-roll inflation followed by a transition to a slow-roll attractor. For transitions without a potential step, we derive an explicit all-order expression for the vertex amplitudes and establish their positivity and dependence on the sharpness of the transition. When a potential step is included, we prove that all nonlinear vertex amplitudes remain positive for upward steps, whereas downward steps allow cancellations at specific parameter values. These cancellations can eliminate entire families of tree contributions, including configurations in which the leading local bispectrum and trispectrum vanish simultaneously while higher order correlations remain nonzero.

Abstract: The European Space Agency's Euclid mission has issued its first major data release(Data Release 1, DR1), providing a transformative view of the extragalactic sky through deep photometry and near-infrared slitless spectroscopy. This paper presents the Euclid DR1 spectroscopic data set and details the evolution of the spectroscopic processing function (SPE PF) pipeline, which is responsible for the automated analysis of 1-dimensional spectra acquired by the Near-Infrared Spectro-Photometer (NISP). The SPE PF pipeline workflow comprises spectroscopic classification, redshift determination, and the systematic measurement of spectral features. Emission line fluxes are derived using two independent methodologies: direct integration (DI) and Gaussian fitting (GF). For sources exhibiting broad H$α$ profiles (velocity dispersion larger than $1500$ km s$^{-1}$), a dedicated `QSO mode' is implemented to robustly deconvolve nuclear and host galaxy components. The data set was validated by cross-matching Euclid results with external spectroscopic catalogues, specifically Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) DR1, and through an extensive visual inspection campaign of approximately 4000 sources within the Euclid Deep Field North (EDF-N). For high-quality samples selected in the EDF-N, we find a 98.5\% agreement between Euclid and DESI spectroscopic redshifts. This success rate increases to 98.9\% when incorporating pipeline probability and reliability estimators. (abridged)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期通过星系四点相关函数（4PCF）观测宇宙学宇称破缺（PV）的尝试，亟需厘清红移空间畸变（RSD）等理论系统误差是否会产生虚假的宇称奇异常信号。

Method: 基于标准欧拉扰动理论（EPT），至三阶密度扰动，结合带偏置示踪体展开与暗物质引力演化，构建相对论性星系数密度；引入局域视线定义以纳入宽角效应，包含潮汐偏差，并推广至任意宇宙背景；将4PCF按球谐函数展开，利用角动量选择定则（Wigner 3-j符号）分析LOS平均后的宇称性质。

Result: 在包括广义相对论修正（O(H/k)）、宽角效应、潮汐偏差和任意背景宇宙学的完整框架下，RSD诱导的各向同性4PCF估计量中的宇称奇异常分量，在对所有视线方向积分（即LOS平均）后严格为零——该结论在平面平行近似和全宽角情形下均成立。

Conclusion: RSD不构成各向同性4PCF中宇称破缺测量的主要系统误差源，因此观测到的宇称奇异常信号更可能反映真实的宇宙学PV物理机制，而非RSD伪影；文中也讨论了该结论的适用范围与潜在的其他PV来源。

Abstract: Recent observational investigation of cosmological Parity Violation (PV) through the galaxy Four-point Correlation Function (4PCF) has motivated a careful theoretical examination of potential systematic contributions. In this work, we demonstrate that parity-odd contributions to the isotropic 4PCF estimator induced by Redshift Space Distortion (RSD), up to the leading order $\mathcal{H}/k$ of general relativistic corrections, vanish upon averaging over all the Line-of-Sight (LOS). We analyse the perturbative relativistic galaxy number density, combining the biased tracer expansion and the dark matter gravitational dynamics within the framework of Standard Eulerian Perturbation Theory (EPT) to third order in the density perturbation. Building on prior studies of the parity-odd mode of the trispectrum (the Fourier-space counterpart of the 4PCF), we extend existing results in three directions: incorporating the wide-angle effect by adopting a local LOS definition for each galaxy in the 4PCF, accounting for tidal bias in the galaxy population, and generalising the assessment to an arbitrary cosmological background. Expanding the 4PCF in spherical harmonics, we show that angular momentum selection rules, encoded in the Wigner 3-j symbols, enforce that only parity-even contributions survive the integration over all LOS orientations, both in the plane-parallel approximation and in the full wide-angle case. This result is crucial for large-scale statistical analyses using isotropic estimators. It supports interpreting possible PV detections as a signal not due to a systematic RSD artefact. We conclude with a discussion of the caveats of our results and identify potential sources of PV.

</details>

Abstract: Supernova cooling provides some of the strongest constraints on light particles beyond the Standard Model, including sub-GeV dark matter (DM). While sufficiently large couplings to the Standard Model sector can trap these particles inside the supernova or cause them to decay before escaping, there typically exists an intermediate range of couplings in which their efficient production and subsequent escape lead to excessive energy loss, resulting in stringent constraints on new light particles. In this work, we demonstrate for the first time that, for certain types of DM-neutrino interactions, resonantly enhanced $s$-channel scattering with neutrinos can efficiently trap DM inside the supernova and can fundamentally alter the conventional supernova cooling constraints. In particular, we show that for mediator masses comparable to the supernova core temperature, this resonant scattering can prevent a substantial fraction of the produced DM from freely escaping, thereby significantly weakening, or even evading, the usual supernova cooling constraints on such models.

Abstract: We study possible signatures of the existence of a Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) critical end-point in low-energy relativistic heavy-ion collisions using two-pion femtoscopy. Within the present analysis, we do not identify an unambiguous signal attributable to the critical end-point. A comparison with simulations employing different equation of state parameters to vary the strength of the critical point, as well as with simulations without a critical end-point, is necessary to establish the sensitivity of these observables to critical phenomena.

In the low-frequency regime, the pair-production spectra are governed by quantum tunneling and temporal interference effects. These degrade statistical power and complicate downstream analyses. In this contribution, we present Exponentiated Subtraction for Matching Events (ESME), a novel approach to NLO+PS matching that yields positive-definite event weights while preserving perturbative accuracy and the logarithmic structure of the shower. Abstract: Over a decade of direct N-body simulations tell us that massive black hole binary (MBHB) merger timescales are strongly dependent on the structural and kinematic properties of the host galaxy merger remnant. Abstract: Most of the hundred million stellar-mass black holes (BHs) expected in the Milky Way are predicted to be isolated. Characterizing this BH population will provide insights into stellar evolution and supernova explosion physics. We combine a Galactic model, stellar population synthesis model, BH initial--final mass relation, and kick velocity distribution to simulate events caused by isolated BH lenses. We assess the expected yield, sensitivity, characterization potential, and signal characteristics of these events to inform GBTDS search strategies. We predict $487 \pm 37$ isolated-BH microlensing events with detectable astrometric signals, 82% of which have impact parameters beyond 2 Einstein radii and consequently have small or undetectable photometric signals. Standard astrometric fits yield unit weight errors > 1.2 for 18% and > 2 for 2% of these events. Sensitivity peaks for 5-6$M_\odot$ BHs and is limited by the five-year GBTDS baseline. Weak microlensing parallax constraints limit BH mass precision, but events with Einstein timescales > 100 days and angular Einstein radii > 1.2 mas have high BH lens probabilities within our Galactic model. Wide-separation astrometric microlensing offers an independent discovery channel for isolated BHs beyond photometrically selected events. Realizing this opportunity requires dedicated multi-season GBTDS searches, joint astrometric and photometric modeling, and accounting for other astrometric variability. Although precise individual masses are challenging for these events, probabilistic lens classification could lead to Galactic abundance constraints on the population of isolated BHs.

We define spheroids as the non-rotating component made up of all stars in retrograde motion, plus an equal number of co-rotating stars mirrored in angular momentum and energy. Abstract: Context. Low-ionization structures (LISs) are small, dense clumps within planetary nebulae (PNe) that stand out in low-ionization emission lines, but the physical processes responsible for their excitation and the nature of H2 remain poorly understood.

We applied H2 excitation diagrams, vibrational and rotational temperature diagnostics, classical near-IR diagnostic diagrams, and spatial profile analysis to constrain the physical conditions of the molecular and atomic gas phases.
  Results. H2, Brg, and [Fe II] emission are detected in all structures studied. The [Fe II]/Brg ratios are well below the values expected for shock or X-ray dominated regions, favoring photoionization as the dominant excitation mechanism of the atomic gas. A clear spatial stratification is observed in both objects, with Brg peaking closer to the central star and H2 1-0S(1) and [Fe II] co-spatial at larger distances. The warm molecular masses derived from H2 emission lines are consistent with those estimated independently from H2 column densities.

The diagnostics presented here support a photoevaporation scenario in which LISs are dense clumps being progressively ionized from outside by the radiation of central stars, while their H2 cores remain self-shielded. Abstract: The circumgalactic media (CGM) of dwarf galaxies are expected to be tightly coupled to their star formation, as low virial temperatures imply a CGM rich in cold accretion and shallow potential wells allow for more effective feedback. We discover a clear transition between the ISM and CGM at 0.033 Rvir, where the Halpha surface brightness profile changes from an exponential to a shallow power law, marking the first such detection at M*/Msun < 10^8. Abstract: Understanding the main drivers of galaxy formation and the connection between observable properties and evolutionary pathways is a central goal of extragalactic astronomy. Observations provide single-epoch snapshots of galaxies at different stages of their development, whereas cosmological simulations allow us to trace the full formation histories of individual systems within a theoretical framework. Extracting information from these datasets remains challenging. Our aim is to use machine-learning techniques to unveil physical properties from the simulation datasets. We apply two unsupervised ML techniques, dimensionality reduction and clustering, to identify galaxy evolution key parameters. Furthermore, using a k-means clustering analysis, we identify six groups of galaxies with distinct evolutionary histories. These groups show systematic differences in stellar mass, morphology, angular momentum, star formation activity, and merger history, and occupy different regions of the SFR-mass and mass-size planes. Our results indicate that the diversity of galaxy formation histories in IllustrisTNG can be organized into a small number of characteristic evolutionary pathways linking galaxy accretion histories and present-day structural and star-forming properties.

Abstract: We investigate the evolution of the emergent spectrum of the Cosmic Dawn dwarf galaxy 2779 using coupled hydrodynamics and photoionization modeling. BPASS stellar population synthesis is coupled with the evolution of the superwind bubble and the surrounding nebular environment. The emergent spectra include escaping stellar radiation, nebular lines and continuum, as well as X-ray emission from the hot superwind cavity. At early stages, the nebular shell suppresses the escape of Lyman-continuum (Lyc) photons, while allowing X-ray emission to emerge. Significant Lyc escape appears near $z \approx 20$, followed by a non-monotonic evolution of the escape fraction ($f_{\text{esc}}$) and the X-ray luminosity per unit star-formation rate ($L_X/\text{SFR}$). Including diffuse ionizing radiation from the nebula can result in an effective escape fraction $f_{\text{esc}} > 1$. However, a more precise determination of stellar mass requires a self-consistent refinement of the star formation history. These models serve as a basis for constructing representative grids of models, as well as for conducting careful checks on whether dwarf galaxies could have driven the reionization of the Universe on their own.

Abstract: Gaia DR3 provides medium-resolution Radial Velocity Spectrograph (RVS) spectra for millions of stars, but the extent to which they contain recoverable cerium (Ce) information across stellar parameter space remains unclear. Ce is reliably detectable primarily in cool giants at [Fe/H] $\gtrsim$ 0.5 dex and with at least [Ce/Fe] $\gtrsim$ 0 at an average SNR of 50, and the detectability region expands toward higher gravities at increasing SNR. Continuum-placement errors of $\gtrsim$1% become a significant source of bias, and at $\gtrsim$2% they completely prevent Ce detection. The criterion we present identifies where RVS Ce abundances, including those from DR4, can be robustly exploited. Outside our detectability region, these abundances should be interpreted cautiously, whether derived by GSP-Spec or by supervised methods. Currently, depending on the assumed continuum-placement uncertainty, between $\sim$40% and $\sim$70% of the published DR3 Ce measurements fall within this region; however, the rest are not reliable. Method: 采用推理框架分析法，围绕四个定性‘无知轴’（观测诊断间接性、恒星种群合成模型需地面真值校准、高红移新观测挑战既有标定、反馈与环境互为因果）展开综述，覆盖电离气体、机械气泡、分子云寿命、星系外流和莱曼连续谱逃逸等主要诊断手段。

Conclusion: 恒星反馈研究正从单一指标测量转向系统性、上下文化解释范式；统一观测标尺（相同示踪物、相同空间尺度）与仿真数据协同是突破关键。

Abstract: Stellar feedback is central to galaxy evolution, yet the coupling of radiation, stellar winds, and supernovae to the multi-phase interstellar medium remains uncertain. This review treats this as a problem of inference, framed around four qualitative axes of ignorance: observational diagnostics are indirect; stellar population synthesis models must be ground-truthed before being used to infer feedback quantities; recent high-redshift observations have opened a new regime that may require re-calibration; and stellar feedback and the affected environment are interdependent. We examine the inference chain, from observables to physical quantities and from those quantities to how energy and momentum are transferred to the surrounding medium, across the principal diagnostics of stellar feedback: ionised gas, mechanically-driven bubbles, giant molecular cloud lifetimes, galactic outflows, and Lyman-continuum escape. We ask how much observations reveal about the link between the feedback-driving massive stars and the feedback-driven interstellar medium. We further discuss how stellar population synthesis assumptions, environment (e.g., metallicity, dust content), and galaxy properties (e.g., stellar mass, gas content) affect feedback measurements, and how high-redshift observations are testing calibrations established in the nearby Universe. The emerging picture is that interpreting a feedback measurement requires explicit control of the inference chain and of its physical context; feedback should therefore be contextualised, i.e. interpreted in terms of mechanism, gas phase, environment, and spatial and temporal scale. Progress will depend on measuring feedback-related quantities across the full range of star-forming systems and, more importantly, on obtaining observations (real, and synthetic from post-processed simulations) that match the same tracer and spatial scale across the same systems.

Abstract: In early-type galaxies, gas that is kinematically misaligned with the stars generally rotates together across all gas phases, implying a single external origin. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于速度矩的方法易受统计涨落影响，且对暗物质模型（如ΛCDM与SIDM）的区分能力有限；需发展更鲁棒、少假设、直接关联可观测量（如半光半径处密度斜率）的建模方法。

Method: 构建球对称Schwarzschild轨道叠加模型；用混合密度网络（MDN）拟合随投影半径平滑变化的视向速度分布高斯混合；完整计入协方差与平滑引入的相关性；采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样暗物质晕参数。

Result: 模拟验证显示半光半径附近的密度轮廓和斜率恢复良好，但速度各向异性存在系统性偏差；对Sculptor、Draco、Ursa Minor的分析表明M_h和S_h对先验选择不敏感，而内渐近斜率γ较敏感；观测推断的三维半光半径及0.5r_h处斜率与FIRE-2 ΛCDM模拟总体一致。

Conclusion: 该框架提升了dSphs动力学建模的稳健性与物理可解释性，为利用恒星运动学区分暗物质模型提供了新途径，但当前数据精度与SIDM模拟样本限制了模型判别能力。

Abstract: We present a spherical Schwarzschild orbit-superposition framework for constraining the dark matter density profiles and stellar orbital structures of the Milky Way dwarf spheroidal galaxies. We model the line-of-sight velocity distribution with a Gaussian mixture whose parameters vary smoothly with projected radius through a mixture density network trained on unbinned stellar velocities. This approach reduces the effect of statistical fluctuations inherent in velocity moments on their kinematic constraints. Their covariance and smoothing-induced correlations are included, and the halo parameters are sampled with Markov Chain Monte Carlo. Mock tests for this model show that the density profiles and slopes near the half-light radius are generally recovered well, although the inferred velocity anisotropy shows some systematic deviations. Applying the model to Sculptor, Draco, and Ursa Minor, we find that the enclosed mass $M_h$ and logarithmic density slope $S_h$ at the projected half-light radius are less sensitive to the adopted two different priors than the asymptotic inner slope $γ$, consistent with earlier studies. We further compare the inferred slopes at the three-dimensional half-light radius $r_h$ and $0.5r_h$ for a given stellar mass with FIRE-2 $Λ$ cold dark matter ($Λ$CDM) and self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) cosmological simulations including baryonic feedback. This comparison avoids introducing halo masses or virial radii that are not directly observable. The inferred slopes are broadly consistent with FIRE-2 $Λ$CDM predictions, although current uncertainties and the limited SIDM sample do not yet allow a robust distinction between dark matter models.

</details>


### [97] [WISE-VAULT: Decade-long Mid-Infrared Variability of WISE AGN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17996)
*Suvendu Rakshit*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用WISE和NEOWISE长达十年的多历元中红外测光数据，研究了约450万个颜色选择AGN的中红外变异性，识别出63万余个变源，并发现其变幅与爱丁顿比呈强反相关，揭示了中红外变异性对吸积物理的重要诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中红外（MIR）变异性是研究活动星系核（AGN）吸积过程和尘埃再处理的有力探针，因其受消光影响小于光学波段。

Method: 基于WISE/NEOWISE超过十年的多历元中红外测光数据，对约450万个颜色选择AGN开展MIR变异性分析；采用最大-最小振幅（ΔW1）、超额方差、约化χ²及结构函数（SF）等方法，在0.5–10年时标上量化变异性。

Result: 识别出632,939个变源，其中54,897个为大振幅变源（ΔW1 > 0.5 mag），624个为极端本征变源（A_var > 0.5 mag）；部分呈现耀发行为，可能是变脸AGN或潮汐撕裂事件候选体；SF分析显示射电探测AGN变幅最高，且变幅与爱丁顿比强负相关、与射电噪度弱正相关。

Conclusion: 中红外变异性是诊断AGN吸积物理的稳健工具，亦可高效识别极端、快速演化的AGN现象；全文发布了变源星表。

Abstract: Mid-infrared (MIR) variability provides a powerful probe of accretion processes and dust reprocessing in active galactic nuclei (AGNs), as it is less affected by extinction than optical flux. Using multi-epoch photometry from \textit{WISE} and NEOWISE spanning over a decade, we investigate the MIR variability of $\sim$4.5 million color-selected AGNs. Variability is quantified using maximum--minimum amplitudes ($ΔW1$), excess variance, reduced $χ^2$, and structure function (SF) analysis over timescales of 0.5--10 years. We identify 632,939 variable AGNs, including 54,897 sources that exhibit large-amplitude variability ($ΔW1 > 0.5$ mag) and 624 with extreme intrinsic variability ($A_{\rm var} > 0.5$ mag). A subset of these sources shows flare-like behavior, identifying them as candidate changing-look AGNs or tidal disruption events that warrant further spectroscopic and multi-wavelength follow-up. The SF analysis shows radio-detected AGNs exhibit the highest variability amplitudes, followed by X-ray detected and the overall AGN sample. The variability amplitude is strongly anti-correlated with the Eddington ratio, indicating enhanced variability in lower accretion rate systems. A weak positive dependence on radio-loudness further suggests a contribution from non-thermal emission in radio sources. These results demonstrate that MIR variability provides a robust diagnostic of accretion physics and an efficient means of identifying extreme and rapidly evolving AGN phenomena. The full catalog of variable AGNs is made publicly available.

</details>


### [98] [Extending an X-ray & Mid-Infrared diagnostic for absorption in Active Galactic Nuclei to higher redshifts using NuSTAR and XMM-Newton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18286)
*Ryan J. Turney,Claire L. Greenwell,David M. Alexander*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了一种基于X射线与中红外光度比（log(L2–10keV/L12μm)）估算AGN柱密度NH的方法至更高红移（z≈0.35），验证其适用性，并据此给出NuSTAR 80个月星表中观测到的Compton厚AGN占比为f_CT = 4.9^{+2.0}_{-2.3}%，与低红移Swift/BAT结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确测定AGN群体中的Compton厚比例（f_CT）对理解超大质量黑洞的演化与增长至关重要；而现有NH测量方法（如谱拟合）受限于高计数要求，需发展适用于中低信噪比源的可靠估算方法。

Method: 基于X射线（2–10 keV）与中红外（12 μm）光度比log(L2–10keV/L12μm)构建NH经验关系，将原适用于z<0.1的本地样本方法外推至z≈0.35；对NSS80中47个亮AGN进行NuSTAR能谱拟合以校准该关系，并用该模型统计推断整个NSS80星表的f_CT。

Result: 高红移关系与本地关系形式一致，仅参数略有差异（源于NSS80样本更高的X射线光度）；由此得到NSS80星表的观测Compton厚分数为f_CT = 4.9^{+2.0}_{-2.3}%（1σ），与Swift/BAT等低-z结果相符。

Conclusion: log(L2–10keV/L12μm)作为NH代理在z≈0.35依然有效，支持其用于中等红移AGN的统计性f_CT研究；该结果强化了当前对AGN遮蔽结构随宇宙时间演化的认知一致性。

Abstract: Determining the Compton thick fraction ($f_{CT}$) within the population of Active Galactic Nuclei (AGN) provides insight into the evolution and growth of Supermassive Black Holes. Measuring $f_{CT}$ requires methods of modelling the AGN column density ($N_{\mathrm{H}}$), typically through spectral fitting which requires high source counts, or estimation methods such as the band ratio. We extend an $N_{\mathrm{H}}$ estimation method using the ratio of observed X-ray and MIR luminosity ($\log\left({L}_{2-10\mathrm{keV}} / {L}_{12\mathrm{μm}}\right)$), previously applied in the local universe ($z<0.1$), to a higher redshift. We used a sample of 47 bright AGN from the NuSTAR 80-month catalogue (NSS80) with a median redshift of $\langle z \rangle = 0.35$ as the high redshift comparison, and we applied X-ray spectral fitting to the NuSTAR sources with physically motivated AGN models to provide $N_\mathrm{H}$ constraints. The high-$z$ relationship was found to be consistent with the same model as the low-$z$ comparison, but with modestly different parameters which we determined to be due to a higher X-ray luminosity within the NSS80 sample. We applied the model to the full NSS80 catalogue to determine a statistical measure of the observed Compton thick fraction at $f_{CT}=4.9^{+2.0}_{-2.3}\%$ to $1σ$ uncertainty, consistent with other recent low-$z$ Swift/BAT measurements.

</details>

### [99] [Emerging understanding of the rare kaon decay $K^+\toπ^+\ell^+\ell^-$ from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17800)
*Zhao-Long Zhang,Xu Feng,Lu-Chang Jin,Bo-Lin Li,Chuan Liu,Christopher T. Sachrajda*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，利用接近物理质量的π介子质量的域壁费米子系综，确定了K+→π+ℓ+ℓ−衰变中矢量形状因子V(z)的符号为负，为标准模型及新物理研究提供了重要依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K+→π+ℓ+ℓ−稀有衰变是检验标准模型和探索新物理的敏感探针，但其实验衰变率仅能确定|V(z)|²，无法确定V(z)的符号；此前格点QCD计算受限于非物理π质量或误差过大，无法解决该符号问题。

Method: 采用两个近物理π质量的域壁费米子系综，发展了一种确保局域与守恒矢量流紫外有限性及连续极限等价性的计算策略；引入四种独立减除方案进行一致性检验，并使用‘单端技巧’降低夸克自圈噪声；在六个类空和类时动量转移点上确定V(z)。

Result: 拟合结果一致显示V(0)<0，在Mηc=1.1 GeV下统计显著性达5.8σ–7.9σ；外推至物理粲夸克质量后，所有拟合形式仍保持负号，显著性为2.0σ–3.4σ。

Conclusion: 本工作首次从第一性原理出发，直接支持V(z)取负号的解，并建立了更高精度格点QCD计算的框架。

Abstract: Rare kaon decays provide sensitive probes of the Standard Model and potential new physics. For $K^+\toπ^+\ell^+\ell^-$, the experimental decay rate determines only the squared vector form factor $|V(z)|^2$, leaving its sign undetermined. Previous lattice QCD calculations have either used unphysical pion masses or suffered from uncertainties too large to resolve this sign. Here we present a first-principles calculation using two domain-wall fermion ensembles with near-physical pion masses. We develop a computational strategy that establishes the ultraviolet finiteness and continuum equivalence of local and conserved vector currents, substantially simplifying the calculation of nonlocal matrix elements. Four independent subtraction schemes provide nontrivial consistency checks, while the one-end trick reduces noise from quark-self-loop contributions. We determine $V(z)$ at six spacelike and timelike momentum transfers. Fits consistently yield $V(0)<0$, with a statistical significance of $5.8σ$--$7.9σ$ at $M_{η_c}=1.1,\mathrm{GeV}$. After extrapolation to the physical charm-quark mass, the negative sign persists across all fit forms, with a significance of $2.0σ$--$3.4σ$. Our results provide direct first-principles evidence favoring the negative-sign solution and establish a framework for more precise lattice QCD determinations.

</details>


### [100] [The ratio of gradient-flow scales $\sqrt{t_0}/w_0$ in $2+1$ flavor lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18038)
*Simon Kuberski,Alejandro Sáez*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过结合Wilson和Zeuthen流数据，利用CLS规范系综，在物理点和连续极限下确定了等味对称2+1味QCD中梯度流尺度比√t₀/w₀的精确值为0.8264(26)，精度达0.31%，有助于统一不同流尺度下的结果并评估已有文献间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一不同梯度流尺度（t₀和w₀）下的物理量标定，解决当前文献中因使用不同流尺度导致的结果不一致问题。

Method: 采用五种格点间距的CLS规范系综，结合Wilson流与Zeuthen流数据，在味SU(3)对称点约束连续外推。

Result: 在Edinburgh共识方案下得到√t₀/w₀ = 0.8264(26)，相对精度约为0.31%。

Conclusion: 该高精度比值为不同流尺度间的结果换算提供了可靠基准，有助于厘清和调和现有QCD非微扰计算中的系统性差异。

Abstract: We determine the ratio $\sqrt{t_0}/w_0$ of gradient-flow scales in isospin-symmetric $2+1$-flavor QCD at the physical point and in the continuum limit. Our analysis uses CLS gauge ensembles at five lattice spacings and combines Wilson and Zeuthen flow data to constrain the continuum extrapolation at the flavor-SU(3)-symmetric point. We obtain $\sqrt{t_0}/w_0=0.8264(26)$ in the Edinburgh consensus scheme, corresponding to a precision of about $0.31\%$. This result relates determinations quoted in terms of either flow scale and helps assess tensions among published results.

</details>
